#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(nbody_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(nbody nbody.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(nbody m)
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {

    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(nbody_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(nbody nbody.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(nbody m)
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
+            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        bodies[k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+    }
+}
+
+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
+#else
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }
+    for (; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
         double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;

-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;

+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {

+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];

+            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        bodies[k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
+    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);
+    int result = 1;
+    try {
+        result = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, numRanks);
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
+        // A failed allocation on one rank must not strand peers in a collective.
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);

+    MPI_Finalize();

+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
+            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        bodies[k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+    }
+}
+
+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;

+    }
+    for (; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
+            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        bodies[k].vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+    }
+}
+
+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
+#else
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }
+    for (; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {

codex
The MPI build runs correctly on four ranks and passes validation. The first comparison found small floating-point differences from SIMD rounding. I’m adjusting the kernel to try to match the original hashes exactly, including when rank partitions are uneven.

+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        // Keep the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[k].vel.z += dvz;
+    }
 }

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
+#else
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }
+    if (i < bodies.size()) {
+        // Use the same SIMD arithmetic for partial blocks so results do not
+        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.
+        Body tail[Batch];
+        const size_t remaining = bodies.size() - i;
+        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(tail, positions);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < remaining; ++k) bodies[i + k].vel = tail[k].vel;
+    }

+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        // Keep the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[k].vel.z += dvz;
+    }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
+#else
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }
+    if (i < bodies.size()) {
+        // Use the same SIMD arithmetic for partial blocks so results do not
+        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.
+        Body tail[Batch];
+        const size_t remaining = bodies.size() - i;
+        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(tail, positions);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < remaining; ++k) bodies[i + k].vel = tail[k].vel;
+    }
+}
+
+void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, std::vector<Vec3>& positions, size_t offset) {
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
+        auto& body = bodies[i];
         body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
         body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
         body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+        positions[offset + i] = body.pos;
     }
 }

+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {

+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

-            Fx += dx * invDist3;

-            Fz += dz * invDist3;

+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        // Keep the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[k].vel.y += dvy;

+    }

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+#else

+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }
+    if (i < bodies.size()) {
+        // Use the same SIMD arithmetic for partial blocks so results do not
+        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.
+        Body tail[Batch];

+        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);

+        for (size_t k = 0; k < remaining; ++k) bodies[i + k].vel = tail[k].vel;

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <exception>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

// The MPI datatypes below transmit these records as consecutive doubles.
static_assert(sizeof(Vec3) == 3 * sizeof(double));
static_assert(sizeof(Body) == 6 * sizeof(double));

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

template <size_t Batch>
void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
    }

    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
#if defined(__GNUC__)
#pragma GCC unroll 1
#endif
        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
        }
    }

    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
        // Keep the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.
        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;
        bodies[k].vel.y += dvy;
        bodies[k].vel.z += dvz;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
#if defined(__AVX512F__)
    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
#else
    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
#endif
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
    }
    if (i < bodies.size()) {
        // Use the same SIMD arithmetic for partial blocks so results do not
        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.
        Body tail[Batch];
        const size_t remaining = bodies.size() - i;
        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);
        computeForceBlock<Batch>(tail, positions);
        for (size_t k = 0; k < remaining; ++k) bodies[i + k].vel = tail[k].vel;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, std::vector<Vec3>& positions, size_t offset) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < bodies.size(); ++i) {
        auto& body = bodies[i];
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
        positions[offset + i] = body.pos;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int runBenchmark(int argc, char** argv, int rank, int numRanks) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            return 1;
        }
    }

    if (numBodies < 0) {
        if (rank == 0) printf("Number of bodies must be nonnegative\n");
        return 1;
    }
    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }

    // Balance complete force rows, including uneven and empty partitions.
    std::vector<int> counts(numRanks), offsets(numRanks);
    int offset = 0;
    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
        counts[r] = numBodies / numRanks + (r < numBodies % numRanks);
        offsets[r] = offset;
        offset += counts[r];
    }
    const int localCount = counts[rank];
    const size_t localOffset = static_cast<size_t>(offsets[rank]);

    // Counts/displacements are in bodies, avoiding overflow from multiplying
    // MPI's int counts by the number of coordinates in each record.
    MPI_Datatype positionType, bodyType;
    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &positionType);
    MPI_Type_commit(&positionType);
    MPI_Type_contiguous(6, MPI_DOUBLE, &bodyType);
    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyType);

    std::vector<Body> bodies(localCount);
    std::vector<Vec3> positions(numBodies);
    std::vector<Body> allBodies;
    if (rank == 0) {
        allBodies.resize(numBodies);
        // Generate the original random sequence once, independent of ranks.
        randomizeBodies(allBodies);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < allBodies.size(); ++i) {
            positions[i] = allBodies[i].pos;
        }
    }
    MPI_Scatterv(allBodies.data(), counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
                 bodies.data(), localCount, bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Bcast(positions.data(), numBodies, positionType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (!printResults && !validate) std::vector<Body>().swap(allBodies);

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies, positions);
        integrateBodies(bodies, positions, localOffset);
        // Only positions are needed by other ranks. In-place gathering sends
        // the updated local segment directly, without packing or velocities.
        // No exchange is needed after the final step.
        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
            MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, positionType, positions.data(),
                           counts.data(), offsets.data(), positionType, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }

    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (rank == 0) printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", static_cast<long>(duration * 1000.0));

    // Preserve global body order for hashes and the reference validation
    // calculation. Ordinary benchmark runs never gather the full state.
    if (printResults || validate) {
        MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data(), localCount, bodyType, allBodies.data(),
                    counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }
    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
    MPI_Type_free(&positionType);

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
        for (const auto& body : allBodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    int result = 0;
    if (validate && rank == 0) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(allBodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(allBodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            result = 1;
        }
    }
    if (validate) MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    return result;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);
    int result = 1;
    try {
        result = runBenchmark(argc, argv, rank, numRanks);
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s\n", rank, error.what());
        // A failed allocation on one rank must not strand peers in a collective.
        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
    }
    MPI_Finalize();
    return result;
}

# Vectorize independent bodies without reassociating force sums or using
# approximate reciprocals. The benchmark does not use math-library errno.
target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -fno-math-errno -Wall -Wextra)

target_link_libraries(nbody PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)

+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {

+        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.

+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }

+        // Use the same SIMD arithmetic for partial blocks so results do not
+        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.
+        Body tail[Batch];

+        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);

+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
+    }
+
+    // SIMD lanes represent different target bodies, so every body's force
+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.
+    for (const Vec3& source : positions) {
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+            const double dx = source.x - x[k];
+            const double dy = source.y - y[k];
+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];
             const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
             const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
             const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {
+        // Keep the separately rounded velocity increments of the original
+        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[k].vel.z += dvz;
+    }
 }

/bin/bash -lc "clang++ -O3 -march=native -std=c++20 -I nbody -x c++ - -o /tmp/nbody_original_clang_r4 <<'NBODY_REFERENCE'
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if ("'!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state'"\\n\");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);
    printf(\"Options:\\n\");
    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");
    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");
    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");
    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);
    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);
    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);
            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.

+        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);

All correctness and argument checks passed. On this machine, the SIMD kernel takes about 1.26 seconds versus 4.96 seconds for the original on 8,192 bodies over 10 steps, with identical hashes. Larger SIMD blocks gave little additional benefit. I’ll keep the compact block size and check MPI scaling.

+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];

+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;

+        bodies[k].vel.z += dvz;

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
+#else
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }

+template <size_t Batch>
+void computeForceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    alignas(64) double x[Batch], y[Batch], z[Batch];
+    alignas(64) double Fx[Batch]{}, Fy[Batch]{}, Fz[Batch]{};
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < Batch; ++k) {

+        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
+        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;

+    // retains the original ascending source order and floating-point sum.

+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif

+            const double dz = source.z - z[k];

+        // kernel; force/integration arithmetic can still use hardware FMA.
+        const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+        const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+        const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+        bodies[k].vel.x += dvx;
+        bodies[k].vel.y += dvy;
+        bodies[k].vel.z += dvz;

+void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+#if defined(__AVX512F__)
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 8;
+#else
+    constexpr size_t Batch = 4;
+#endif
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; i + Batch <= bodies.size(); i += Batch) {
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(bodies.data() + i, positions);
+    }
+    if (i < bodies.size()) {
+        // Use the same SIMD arithmetic for partial blocks so results do not
+        // depend on which rank (or SIMD lane) owns a body.
+        Body tail[Batch];

+        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);
+        computeForceBlock<Batch>(tail, positions);
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < remaining; ++k) bodies[i + k].vel = tail[k].vel;

codex
Implemented mandatory MPI support in the two existing files. The executable remains `nbody`, with balanced partitions, SIMD force calculations, and position-only exchanges.

Verified identical original hashes and validation across 1–8 ranks, including uneven and empty partitions. For 8,192 bodies over 10 steps, local simulation time improved from 4.96 s to 0.16 s on eight ranks. Multi-node performance remains untested.

+int main(int argc, char** argv) {

Implemented mandatory MPI support in the two existing files. The executable remains `nbody`, with balanced partitions, SIMD force calculations, and position-only exchanges.

Verified identical original hashes and validation across 1–8 ranks, including uneven and empty partitions. For 8,192 bodies over 10 steps, local simulation time improved from 4.96 s to 0.16 s on eight ranks. Multi-node performance remains untested.
